Clone the repository using the project's Git URL.\n2. Navigate to the project directory.\n3. Install dependencies: `npm install`.\n4. Install the Base44 CLI: `npm install -g base44@latest`.\n\nSee the [Base44 CLI docs](https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/get-started/overview) if you want to run Base44 commands directly.\n\n## Run Locally\n\nRun the full local development environment from the project root:\n\n```bash\nbase44 dev\n```\n\n`base44 dev` starts the local Base44 development backend and, when this app is configured for it, also starts the frontend dev server for you. Use the frontend URL printed by the command.\n\nFor example, when the Base44 project config includes a `serveCommand`, `base44 dev` can launch the frontend too:\n\n```json5\n{\n  \"site\": {\n    \"serveCommand\": \"npm run dev\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\nIn a Base44 project this lives in `base44/config.jsonc`.\n\n## Run Only The Frontend\n\nIf you only want to work on the frontend against the hosted Base44 backend, run:\n\n```bash\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the local URL printed by Vite.\n\n## Use The Hosted Backend\n\nFor frontend-only development, create or update `.env.local` in the project root:\n\n```bash\nVITE_BASE44_APP_ID=your_app_id\nVITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL=https://your-app.base44.app\n```\n\n`VITE_BASE44_APP_ID` identifies the Base44 app.\n\n`VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL` tells the Base44 Vite plugin where to send local `/api` requests. Point it at your deployed Base44 app URL when you want the local frontend to use the hosted backend.\n\nWhen you use `base44 dev`, the command injects the local Base44 values for you, so `.env.local` is mainly needed for frontend-only workflows.\n\n## Publish Your Changes\n\nAfter pushing your changes to git, open the Base44 dashboard and publish the app:\n\n```bash\nbase44 dashboard open\n```\n\n## Docs & Support\n\nDocumentation: [https://docs.base44.com/Integrations/Using-GitHub](https://docs.base44.com/Integrations/Using-GitHub)\n\nBase44 CLI command reference: [https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/commands/introduction](https://docs.base44.com/developers/references/cli/commands/introduction)\n\nSupport: [https://app.base44.com/support](https://app.base44.com/support)\n",

        "base44/shared/supabase.ts": "import { secrets } from \"base44:runtime\";\n\nconst BUCKET = \"phone-images\";\n\nexport function sbConfig() {\n  const url = (secrets.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\") || \"\").replace(/\\/$/, \"\");\n  const key = secrets.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\") || \"\";\n  if (!url || !key) throw new Error(\"SUPABASE_URL / SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY nicht konfiguriert\");\n  return { url, key };\n}\n\nexport function slugify(s) {\n  const v = String(s || \"\").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, \"\").slice(0, 60);\n  return v || \"item\";\n}\n\nexport function extFromUrl(u) {\n  try {\n    const m = String(u).match(/\\.(png|jpg|jpeg|webp|gif)(\\?|#|$)/i);\n    return m ? m[1].toLowerCase().replace(\"jpeg\", \"jpg\") : \"jpg\";\n  } catch { return \"jpg\"; }\n}\n\nfunction headers(key, extra = {}) {\n  return { apikey: key, Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", ...extra };\n}\n\nexport async function sbList(table, opts = {}) {\n  const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n  const p = new URLSearchParams();\n  p.set(\"select\", opts.select || \"*\");\n  if (opts.order) p.set(\"order\", opts.order);\n  if (opts.limit) p.set(\"limit\", String(opts.limit));\n  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?${p.toString()}`, { headers: headers(key) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`list ${table} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  return await res.json();\n}\n\nexport async function sbFilter(table, filters, opts = {}) {\n  const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n  const p = new URLSearchParams();\n  p.set(\"select\", opts.select || \"*\");\n  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(filters || {})) {\n    if (v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== \"\") p.set(k, v);\n  }\n  if (opts.order) p.set(\"order\", opts.order);\n  if (opts.limit) p.set(\"limit\", String(opts.limit));\n  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?${p.toString()}`, { headers: headers(key) });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`filter ${table} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  return await res.json();\n}\n\nexport async function sbInsertOne(table, row) {\n  const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: headers(key, { Prefer: \"return=representation\" }),\n    body: JSON.stringify(row),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`insert ${table} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  const arr = await res.json();\n  return arr && arr[0];\n}\n\nexport async function sbUpdate(table, id, values) {\n  const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {\n    method: \"PATCH\",\n    headers: headers(key, { Prefer: \"return=representation\" }),\n    body: JSON.stringify(values),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`update ${table} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  const arr = await res.json();\n  return arr && arr[0];\n}\n\nexport async function sbDelete(table, id) {\n  const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?id=eq.${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {\n    method: \"DELETE\",\n    headers: headers(key),\n  });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`delete ${table} ${res.status}: ${await res.text()}`);\n  return { success: true };\n}\n\nexport async function uploadImageToSupabase(sourceUrl, objectPath) {\n  if (!sourceUrl) return sourceUrl;\n  try {\n    const { url, key } = sbConfig();\n    const imgRes = await fetch(sourceUrl);\n    if (!imgRes.ok) throw new Error(`fetch ${imgRes.status}`);\n    const blob = await imgRes.blob();\n    const ct = blob.type || \"image/jpeg\";\n    const buf = await blob.arrayBuffer();\n    const upRes = await fetch(`${url}/storage/v1/object/${BUCKET}/${objectPath}`, {\n      method: \"PUT\",\n      headers: { apikey: key, Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`, \"Content-Type\": ct },\n      body: buf,\n    });\n    if (!upRes.ok) throw new Error(`upload ${upRes.status}`);\n    return `${url}/storage/v1/object/public/${BUCKET}/${objectPath}`;\n  } catch (e) {\n    return sourceUrl;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function sortIphonesNewest(arr, nameKey = \"model_name\") {\n  const key = (name) => {\n    const n = String(name || \"\").toLowerCase().trim();\n    let gen = -1, sub = 0;\n    const m = n.match(/iphone\\s+(\\d+)/);\n    if (m) gen = parseInt(m[1], 10);\n    else if (/\\biphone\\s*x[sr]?\\b/.test(n)) gen = 10;\n    else if (/\\bse\\b/.test(n)) {\n      gen = -100;\n      const sm = n.match(/se\\s*(\\d+)/);\n      sub = sm ? parseInt(sm[1], 10) : 0;\n    }\n    let v = 1;\n    if (/pro max/.test(n)) v = 5;\n    else if (/pro/.test(n)) v = 4;\n    else if (/plus/.test(n)) v = 3;\n    else if (/mini/.test(n)) v = 0;\n    return { gen, sub, v };\n  };\n  return [...(arr || [])].sort((a, b) => {\n    const ka = key((a && (a[nameKey] || a.model_name)) || \"\");\n    const kb = key((b && (b[nameKey] || b.model_name)) || \"\");\n    if (kb.gen !== ka.gen) return kb.gen - ka.gen;\n    if (kb.sub !== ka.sub) return kb.sub - ka.sub;\n    return kb.v - ka.v;\n  });\n}",

        "src/hooks/use-size.jsx": "import * as React from \"react\"\n\nexport function useSize(ref) {\n  const [size, setSize] = React.useState(null)\n\n  // useLayoutEffect (not useEffect): the initial measurement must land before\n  // the browser paints, so consumers can render their real content on the\n  // very first painted frame instead of a guess. A ResizeObserver's first\n  // callback arrives too late for that — by then an <img> src guess has\n  // already been dispatched to the network.\n  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {\n    const element = ref.current\n    if (!element) return\n\n    const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect()\n    setSize({ width: rect.width, height: rect.height })\n\n    const observer = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => {\n      const { width, height } = entry.contentRect\n      setSize({ width, height })\n    })\n\n    observer.observe(element)\n    return () => observer.disconnect()\n  }, [ref])\n\n  return size\n}\n",

        "src/lib/authReturnTo.js": "// Shared by the auth pages (Login, Register, and any page that resumes a flow\n// after sign-in, e.g. the MCP OAuth consent page). Keep the redirect\n// validation in one place — it is security-sensitive and easy to drift.\n\n// Resolve ?returnTo= to a safe same-origin path, else \"/\".\n//\n// The same-origin check alone is not enough: a value like /.//evil.com or\n// /\\evil.com parses same-origin but normalizes to a protocol-relative\n// //evil.com when assigned to location.href — an open redirect. So require the\n// resolved path to be exactly one leading slash (no \"//\" prefix, no backslash).\nexport function safeReturnTo() {\n  const raw = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(\"returnTo\");\n  if (!raw) return \"/\";\n  try {\n    const url = new URL(raw, window.location.origin);\n    if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return \"/\";\n    // Strip app-bootstrap params: app-params.js persists these from the URL into\n    // localStorage before the SDK initializes, so a crafted returnTo could\n    // otherwise poison the freshly issued session — repointing the app at an\n    // attacker's backend (app_base_url/app_id/functions_version) or overwriting\n    // the token. Normal app-flow params (e.g. the OAuth consent ctx) are kept.\n    // The full app-params.js bootstrap set (src/lib/app-params.js) — any of\n    // these in a crafted returnTo would be persisted at next load.\n    for (const p of [\"access_token\", \"clear_access_token\", \"app_id\", \"app_base_url\", \"functions_version\", \"from_url\"]) {\n      url.searchParams.delete(p);\n    }\n    const path = url.pathname + url.search;\n    if (!path.startsWith(\"/\") || path.startsWith(\"//\") || path.includes(\"\\\\\")) return \"/\";\n    return path;\n  } catch {\n    return \"/\";\n  }\n}\n",
        "src/lib/AuthContext.jsx": "import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';\nimport { appParams } from '@/lib/app-params';\nimport { createAxiosClient } from '@base44/sdk/dist/utils/axios-client';\n\nconst AuthContext = createContext();\n\nexport const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {\n  const [user, setIsLoadingPublicSettings] = useState(true);\n  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState(null);\n  const [authChecked, setAuthChecked] = useState(false);\n  const [appPublicSettings, setAppPublicSettings] = useState(null); // Contains only { id, public_settings }\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    checkAppState();\n  }, []);\n\n  const checkAppState = async () => {\n    try {\n      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(true);\n      setAuthError(null);\n      \n      // First, check app public settings (with token if available)\n      // This will tell us if auth is required, user not registered, etc.\n      const appClient = createAxiosClient({\n        baseURL: `/api/apps/public`,\n        headers: {\n          'X-App-Id': appParams.appId\n        },\n        token: appParams.token, // Include token if available\n        interceptResponses: true\n      });\n      \n      try {\n        const publicSettings = await appClient.get(`/prod/public-settings/by-id/${appParams.appId}`);\n        setAppPublicSettings(publicSettings);\n        \n        // If we got the app public settings successfully, check if user is authenticated\n        if (appParams.token) {\n          await checkUserAuth();\n        } else {\n          setIsLoadingAuth(false);\n          setIsAuthenticated(false);\n          setAuthChecked(true);\n        }\n        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);\n      } catch (appError) {\n        console.error('App state check failed:', appError);\n        \n        // Handle app-level errors\n        if (appError.status === 403 && appError.data?.extra_data?.reason) {\n          const reason = appError.data.extra_data.reason;\n          if (reason === 'auth_required') {\n            setAuthError({\n              type: 'auth_required',\n              message: 'Authentication required'\n            });\n          } else if (reason === 'user_not_registered') {\n            setAuthError({\n              type: 'user_not_registered',\n              message: 'User not registered for this app'\n            });\n          } else {\n            setAuthError({\n              type: reason,\n              message: appError.message\n            });\n          }\n        } else {\n          setAuthError({\n            type: 'unknown',\n            message: appError.message || 'Failed to load app'\n          });\n        }\n        setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);\n        setIsLoadingAuth(false);\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('Unexpected error:', error);\n      setAuthError({\n        type: 'unknown',\n        message: error.message || 'An unexpected error occurred'\n      });\n      setIsLoadingPublicSettings(false);\n      setIsLoadingAuth(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const checkUserAuth = async () => {\n    try {\n      // Now check if the user is authenticated\n      setIsLoadingAuth(true);\n      const currentUser = await base44.auth.me();\n      setUser(currentUser);\n      setIsAuthenticated(true);\n      setIsLoadingAuth(false);\n      setAuthChecked(true);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('User auth check failed:', error);\n      setIsLoadingAuth(false);\n      setIsAuthenticated(false);\n      setAuthChecked(true);\n      \n      // If user auth fails, it might be an expired token\n      if (error.status === 401 || error.status === 403) {\n        setAuthError({\n          type: 'auth_required',\n          message: 'Authentication required'\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  const logout = (shouldRedirect = true) => {\n    setUser(null);\n    setIsAuthenticated(false);\n    \n    if (shouldRedirect) {\n      // Use the SDK's logout method which handles token cleanup and redirect\n      base44.auth.logout(window.location.href);\n    } else {\n      // Just remove the token without redirect\n      base44.auth.logout();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const navigateToLogin = () => {\n    // Use the SDK's redirectToLogin method\n    base44.auth.redirectToLogin(window.location.href);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ \n      user, \n      isAuthenticated, \n      isLoadingAuth,\n      isLoadingPublicSettings,\n      authError,\n      appPublicSettings,\n      authChecked,\n      logout,\n      navigateToLogin,\n      checkUserAuth,\n      checkAppState\n    }}>\n      {children}\n    </AuthContext.Provider>\n  );\n};\n\nexport const useAuth = () => {\n  const context = useContext(AuthContext);\n  if (!context) {\n    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');\n  }\n  return context;\n};\n",

        "src/lib/app-params.js": "const isNode = typeof window === 'undefined';\nconst windowObj = isNode ? { localStorage: new Map() } : window;\nconst storage = windowObj.localStorage;\n\nconst toSnakeCase = (str) => {\n\treturn str.replace(/([A-Z])/g, '_$1').toLowerCase();\n}\n\nconst getAppParamValue = (paramName, { defaultValue = undefined, removeFromUrl = false } = {}) => {\n\tif (isNode) {\n\t\treturn defaultValue;\n\t}\n\tconst storageKey = `base44_${toSnakeCase(paramName)}`;\n\tconst urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n\tconst searchParam = urlParams.get(paramName);\n\tif (removeFromUrl) {\n\t\turlParams.delete(paramName);\n\t\tconst newUrl = `${window.location.pathname}${urlParams.toString() ? `?${urlParams.toString()}` : \"\"\n\t\t\t}${window.location.hash}`;\n\t\twindow.history.replaceState({}, document.title, newUrl);\n\t}\n\tif (searchParam) {\n\t\tstorage.setItem(storageKey, searchParam);\n\t\treturn searchParam;\n\t}\n\tif (defaultValue) {\n\t\tstorage.setItem(storageKey, defaultValue);\n\t\treturn defaultValue;\n\t}\n\tconst storedValue = storage.getItem(storageKey);\n\tif (storedValue) {\n\t\treturn storedValue;\n\t}\n\treturn null;\n}\n\nconst getAppParams = () => {\n\tif (getAppParamValue(\"clear_access_token\") === 'true') {\n\t\tstorage.removeItem('base44_access_token');\n\t\tstorage.removeItem('token');\n\t}\n\treturn {\n\t\tappId: getAppParamValue(\"app_id\", { defaultValue: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_ID }),\n\t\ttoken: getAppParamValue(\"access_token\", { removeFromUrl: true }),\n\t\tfromUrl: getAppParamValue(\"from_url\", { defaultValue: window.location.href }),\n\t\tfunctionsVersion: getAppParamValue(\"functions_version\", { defaultValue: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_FUNCTIONS_VERSION }),\n\t\tappBaseUrl: getAppParamValue(\"app_base_url\", { defaultValue: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL }),\n\t}\n}\n\n\nexport const appParams = {\n\t...getAppParams()\n}\n",

        "src/pages/OAuthConsent.jsx": "import React, { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { appParams } from \"@/lib/app-params\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { ShieldCheck, Loader2 } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport AuthLayout from \"@/components/AuthLayout\";\n\n// App-side OAuth consent page for the app's MCP server. The platform redirects\n// AI clients here (see base44/mcp/config.json `consent_path`) with an opaque\n// `ctx` handle — the authorization request itself lives on the server. This page\n// gates on the app-user session, fetches the display info for that handle, shows\n// the categories of access being granted, and posts the approve/deny decision.\n// Do not change the fetch calls, headers, or the `ctx` handle handling — styling\n// and copy are safe to edit.\nexport default function OAuthConsent() {\n  const ctx = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(\"ctx\");\n  const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);\n  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [decided, setDecided] = useState(\"\");\n  const [error, setError] = useState(\"\");\n  const [reconnect, setReconnect] = useState(\"\");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      let redirecting = false;\n      try {\n        if (!ctx) {\n          setError(\"This authorization link is invalid or has expired.\");\n          return;\n        }\n        // Resolve the handle first: a dead handle must never render\n        // approve/deny, and the response carries the app's configured login\n        // route for the signed-out redirect below. Send the session (cookie +\n        // bearer token) so the server can list the granted tools for a\n        // signed-in user — the same auth the approve/deny call sends; without\n        // it the display request is anonymous and shows no tools.\n        const infoHeaders = {};\n        if (appParams.token) infoHeaders.Authorization = \"Bearer \" + appParams.token;\n        const res = await fetch(\n          `/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/consent-info?handle=${encodeURIComponent(ctx)}`,\n          { credentials: \"include\", headers: infoHeaders },\n        );\n        if (!res.ok) {\n          setError(\"This authorization link is invalid or has expired.\");\n          return;\n        }\n        const data = await res.json();\n        // Gate on the server's auth result, NOT base44.auth.isAuthenticated():\n        // the SDK check runs the bearer path, so a cookie-only session (platform\n        // login/SSO, or a private app with a stale localStorage token) would read\n        // as signed-out and redirect — even though /consent-info just\n        // authenticated this same request via its cookie fallback. data.authenticated\n        // keeps the redirect decision in agreement with what the server returned.\n        if (!data.authenticated) {\n          // The short handle rides back in returnTo; login_path is\n          // owner-configured and validated server-side as a same-origin path.\n          // Send from_url too: a custom-auth app coerced to platform auth (e.g.\n          // public_without_login under workspace SSO) serves the platform login,\n          // which honors from_url rather than returnTo. Rebuild the query from\n          // `ctx` alone — never forward window.location.search raw: the platform\n          // resume returns from_url verbatim, so crafted extras on the consent\n          // link (app_base_url, access_token, …) would ride through the login\n          // round-trip and app-params.js would persist them into the freshly\n          // authenticated session.\n          const returnTo =\n            window.location.pathname + \"?ctx=\" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);\n          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);\n          redirecting = true; // keep the spinner while the browser navigates\n          window.location.href =\n            (data.login_path || \"/login\") + \"?returnTo=\" + encoded + \"&from_url=\" + encoded;\n          return;\n        }\n        setInfo(data);\n      } catch (e) {\n        setError(\"Could not load this authorization request. Please try again.\");\n      } finally {\n        if (!redirecting) setChecking(false);\n      }\n    })();\n  }, [ctx]);\n\n  const respond = async (action) => {\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    setError(\"\");\n    try {\n      const headers = { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" };\n      // Cookie-backed sessions carry no token; sending \"Bearer null\" would\n      // shadow the valid cookie, so add the header only when a token exists.\n      if (appParams.token) headers.Authorization = \"Bearer \" + appParams.token;\n      const res = await fetch(`/api/apps/${appParams.appId}/mcp/authorize-grant`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        credentials: \"include\",\n        headers,\n        body: JSON.stringify({ ctx, action }),\n      });\n      if (!res.ok) {\n        // 401 = the session expired before the (single-use, still-unconsumed)\n        // handle was spent; retrying the same controls re-sends the dead session\n        // forever. Send the user back through login preserving `ctx` — the same\n        // redirect the initial signed-out path uses — so they can return and\n        // approve the still-valid handle.\n        if (res.status === 401) {\n          const returnTo = window.location.pathname + \"?ctx=\" + encodeURIComponent(ctx);\n          const encoded = encodeURIComponent(returnTo);\n          window.location.href =\n            ((info && info.login_path) || \"/login\") + \"?returnTo=\" + encoded + \"&from_url=\" + encoded;\n          return;\n        }\n        // These all come AFTER the single-use handle is atomically consumed\n        // (409 tool set changed; 403 host/resource/app mismatch; 404 access\n        // gone; 400 malformed/handle already used), so retrying can only 404.\n        // Show a terminal reconnect state, not an impossible \"try again\".\n        if ([400, 403, 404, 409].includes(res.status)) {\n          let detail = \"\";\n          try { detail = (await res.json()).detail; } catch (_) { /* keep default */ }\n          setReconnect(detail || \"This authorization can no longer be completed. Reconnect from your AI client to try again.\");\n          setSubmitting(false);\n          return;\n        }\n        throw new Error(\"Could not complete authorization. Please try again.\");\n      }\n      const data = await res.json();\n      window.location.href = data.redirect_url;\n      if (!/^https?:/i.test(data.redirect_url)) {\n        // Custom-scheme redirect (native AI clients, e.g. cursor://): browsers\n        // may block or not visibly navigate, so show a terminal state instead\n        // of an eternal spinner.\n        setDecided(action);\n        setSubmitting(false);\n      }\n    } catch (e) {\n      setError(e.message);\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (checking) {\n    return (\n      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title=\"Authorize access\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-6 text-muted-foreground\">\n          <Loader2 className=\"w-5 h-5 mr-2 animate-spin\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n          Loading…\n        </div>\n      </AuthLayout>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const client = (info && info.client_name) || \"An AI client\";\n  const appName = (info && info.app_name) || \"this app\";\n\n  if (decided) {\n    return (\n      <AuthLayout\n        icon={ShieldCheck}\n        title={decided === \"approve\" ? \"Access granted\" : \"Access denied\"}\n        subtitle={`You can return to ${client} and close this window.`}\n      />\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Terminal: the authorization request is no longer valid (tool set changed +\n  // handle consumed). Retrying can't succeed, so show reconnect guidance with\n  // no approve/deny controls.\n  if (reconnect) {\n    return (\n      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title=\"Reconnect required\">\n        <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm\">\n          {reconnect}\n        </div>\n      </AuthLayout>\n    );\n  }\n\n  // No consent details means nothing trustworthy to approve: a failed\n  // consent-info load (expired handle, rate limit, transient error) renders\n  // the error alone, never the approve/deny controls.\n  if (error && !info) {\n    return (\n      <AuthLayout icon={ShieldCheck} title=\"Authorize access\">\n        <div className=\"p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm\">\n          {error}\n        </div>\n      </AuthLayout>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const tools = Array.isArray(info.tools) ? info.tools : [];\n\n  return (\n    <AuthLayout\n      icon={ShieldCheck}\n      title=\"Authorize access\"\n      subtitle={`${client} wants to access ${appName} on your behalf`}\n    >\n      {error && (\n        <div className=\"mb-4 p-3 rounded-lg bg-destructive/10 text-destructive text-sm\">\n          {error}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <p className=\"text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-2\">\n        {tools.length ? `It will be able to use these tools in ${appName}:` : \"No tools requested\"}\n      </p>\n      {tools.length > 0 && (\n        <ul className=\"space-y-2 text-sm mb-6\">\n          {tools.map((tool) => (\n            <li key={tool.name} className=\"flex flex-col\">\n              <span className=\"text-foreground font-medium\">\n                {tool.title || tool.name}\n              </span>\n              {tool.description && (\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{tool.description}</span>\n              )}\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n        <Button\n          variant=\"outline\"\n          className=\"flex-1 h-12 font-medium\"\n          disabled={submitting}\n          onClick={() => respond(\"deny\")}\n        >\n          Deny\n        </Button>\n        <Button\n          className=\"flex-1 h-12 font-medium\"\n          disabled={submitting}\n          onClick={() => respond(\"approve\")}\n        >\n          {submitting ? <Loader2 className=\"w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin\" /> : null}\n          Approve\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    </AuthLayout>\n  );\n}\n",

}, []);\n\n  const openNew = () => {\n    setEditing(null);\n    setForm({ model_name: '', model_code: '', image_url: '', is_hidden: false, image_url_upload: '' });\n    setOpen(true);\n  };\n  const openEdit = (item) => {\n    setEditing(item);\n    setForm({ model_name: item.model_name || '', model_code: item.model_code || '', image_url: item.image_url || '', is_hidden: !!item.is_hidden, image_url_upload: '' });\n    setOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const pickImage = async (e) => {\n    const f = e.target.files?.[0];\n    if (!f) return;\n    const { file_url } = await base44.integrations.Core.UploadFile({ file: f });\n    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, image_url: file_url, image_url_upload: file_url }));\n  };\n\n  const save = async (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      const payload = { ...form };\n      if (editing) await base44.functions.invoke('sbModels', { method: 'update', id: editing.id, data: payload });\n      else await base44.functions.invoke('sbModels', { method: 'create', data: payload });\n      setOpen(false);\n      load();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const del = async (item) => {\n    if (!confirm(`Modell \"${item.model_name}\" wirklich löschen? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-slate-300'}`} />\n                </div>\n                <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5\">{!m.is_hidden ? 'Sichtbar' : 'Versteckt'}</p>\n                {m.model_code && <p className=\"text-[0.7rem] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-1 truncate\">{m.model_code}</p>}\n                <div className=\"flex gap-2 mt-3\">\n                  <button onClick={() => openEdit(m)} className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 inline-flex items-center gap-1\"><Pencil className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />Bearbeiten</button>\n                  <button onClick={() => del(m)} className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700 inline-flex items-center gap-1\"><Trash2 className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />Löschen</button>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {items.length === 0 && (\n            <div className=\"col-span-full text-center py-16 text-slate-400\">\n              <Smartphone className=\"w-10 h-10 mx-auto mb-2\" />Noch keine Modelle.\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>\n        <DialogContent className=\"sm:max-w-lg\">\n          <DialogHeader>\n            <DialogTitle>{editing ?         "src/pages/admin/Kategorien.jsx": "import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';\nimport { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';\nimport { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';\nimport { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';\nimport {\n  Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter\n} from '@/components/ui/dialog';\nimport { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, Loader2, Tag } from 'lucide-react';\n\nexport default function Kategorien() {\n  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);\n  const [usage, setUsage] = useState({});\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', category: '' });\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  const load = async () => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      const [pmRes, dpRes] = await Promise.all([\n        base44.functions.invoke('sbCategories', { method: 'list' }),\n        base44.functions.invoke('sbParts', { method: 'list' }),\n      ]);\n      const master = pmRes.data || [];\n      setItems(master);\n      const u = {};\n      (dpRes.data || []).forEach((dp) => { if (dp.part_id) u[dp.part_id] = (u[dp.part_id] || 0) + 1; });\n      setUsage(u);\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);\n\n  const categoryGroups = useMemo(() => {\n    const g = {};\n    items.forEach((p) => { const c = p.category || 'Ohne Kategorie'; B. Display, Akku) für die Zuordnung zu Geräten</p>\n        </div>\n        <Button onClick={openNew} className=\"bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700\">\n          <Plus className=\"w-4 h-4 mr-2\" />Neuer Teiltyp\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n\n      {loading ? (\n        <div className=\"py-20 text-center text-slate-400\"><Loader2 className=\"w-6 h-6 animate-spin mx-auto\" /></div>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n className=\"mt-3 text-sm text-slate-600\">\n                      <span className=\"font-bold text-emerald-600\">{usage[c.id] || 0}</span> Ersatzteile\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                ))}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n          {items.length === 0 && (\n            <div className=\"text-center py-16 text-slate-400\">\n         "src/pages/admin/Ersatzteile.jsx": "import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';\nimport { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';\nimport { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';\nimport { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';\nimport {\n  Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter\n} from '@/components/ui/dialog';\nimport { Plus, Pencil, Trash2, Loader2, Package, Search, Upload } from 'lucide-react';\n\nfunction formatPrice(val, currency) {\n  const n = Number(val || 0).toFixed(2).replace('.', ',');\n  return currency && currency !== 'EUR' ? `${n} ${currency}` : `${n} €`;\n}\n\nexport default function Ersatzteile() {\n  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);\n  const [devices, setDevices] = useState([]);\n  const [partsMaster, setPartsMaster] = useState([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [filterDevice, setFilterDevice] = useState('');\n  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('');\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ device_id: '', part_id: '', part_name_custom: '', price: '', currency: 'EUR', quality_grade: '', image_url: '', image_url_upload: '' });\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  const load = async () => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      const [dpRes, devRes, pmRes] = await Promise.all([\n        base44.functions.invoke('sbParts', { method: 'list' }),\n        base44.functions.invoke('sbModels', { method: 'list' }),\n        base44.functions.invoke('sbCategories', { method: 'list' }),\n      ]);\n      setItems(dpRes.data || []);\n      setDevices(devRes.data || []);\n      setPartsMaster(pmRes.data || []);\n    } finally {\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);\n\n  const deviceMap = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(devices.map((d) => [d.id, d.model_name])), [devices]);\n  const partMap = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(partsMaster.map((p) => [p.id, p.name])), [partsMaster]);\n  const typeOptions = useMemo(() => [...new Set(partsMaster.map((p) => p.category).filter(Boolean))].sort(), [partsMaster]);\n\n  const partName = (p) => p.part_name_custom || partMap[p.part_id] || 'Ersatzteil';\n  const partType = (p) => {\n    const master = partsMaster.find((m) => m.id === p.part_id);\n    return master ? master.category : '';\n  };\n\n  const filtered = items.filter((p) => {\n    if (filterDevice && p.device_id !== Number(filterDevice)) return false;\n    if (filterType && partType(p) !== filterType) return false;\n    if (query && !partName(p).toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())) return false;\n    return true;\n  });\n\n  const openNew = () => {\n    setEditing(null);\n    setForm({ device_id: filterDevice || (devices[0] && devices[0].id) || '', part_id: partsMaster[0] && partsMaster[0].id || '', part_name_custom: '', price: '', currency: 'EUR', quality_grade: '', image_url: '', image_url_upload: '' });\n    setOpen(true);\n  };\n  const openEdit = (item) => {\n    setEditing(item);\n    setForm({ device_id: item.device_id || '', part_id: item.part_id || '', part_name_custom: item.part_name_custom || '', price: item.price !== null && item.price !== undefined ? String(item.price) : '', currency: item.currency || 'EUR', quality_grade: item.quality_grade || '', image_url: item.image_url || '', image_url_upload: '' });\n    setOpen(true);\n  };\n\n  const pickImage = async (e) => {\n    const f = e.target.files?.[0];\n    if (!f) return;\n    const { file_url } = await base44.integrations.Core.UploadFile({ file: f });\n    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, image_url: file_url, image_url_upload: file_url }));\n  };\n\n  const save = async (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      const payload = { ...form, price: form.price === '' ? null : form.price };\n      if (editing) await base44.functions.invoke('sbParts', { method: 'update', id: editing.id, data: payload });\n      else await base44.functions.invoke('sbParts', { method: 'create', data: payload });\n      setOpen(false);\n      load();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const del = async (item) => {\n    if (!confirm(`Ersatzteil \"${partName(item)}\" (${deviceMap[item.device_id] || '—'}) wirklich löschen?`)) return;\n    await base44.functions.invoke('sbParts', { method: 'delete', id: item.id });\n    load();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-5\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3\">\n        <div>\n          <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900\">Ersatzteile</h1>\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-500\">{filtered.length} von {items.length} preisbewerteten Teilen</p>\n        </div>\n        <Button onClick={openNew} className=\"bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700\">\n          <Plus className=\"w-4 h-4 mr-2\" />Neues Ersatzteil\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3\">\n        <div className=\"relative flex-1\">\n          <Search className=\"w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 Modelle</option>\n          {devices.map((d) => <option key={d.id} value={d.id}>{d.model_name}</option>)}\n        </select>\n        <select value={filterType} onChange={(e) => )}\n                        <span className=\"font-semibold text-slate-900\">{partName(p)}</span>\n                      </div>\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-slate-600 hidden sm:table-cell\">{deviceMap[p.device_id] || '—'}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 hidden sm:table-cell text-slate-600\">{partType(p) || '—'}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\"><span className=\"text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-2 py-1 rounded-md\">{p.quality_grade || '—'}</span></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 font-bold text-emerald-600\">{formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-right\">\n                      <div </div>\n                    </td>\n                  </tr>\n                ))}\n                {filtered.length === 0 && (\n                  <tr><td colSpan={6} className=\"text-center py-12         "src/components/ScrollToTop.jsx": "import { useEffect } from \"react\";\nimport { useLocation, useNavigationType } from \"react-router-dom\";\n\nconst getHashId = (hash) => {\n  const rawId = hash.slice(1);\n\n  try {\n    return decodeURIComponent(rawId);\n  } catch {\n    return rawId;\n  }\n};\n\nexport default function ScrollToTop() {\n  const { pathname, hash } = useLocation();\n  const navigationType = useNavigationType();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (navigationType === \"POP\") return;\n\n    if (hash) {\n      const id = getHashId(hash);\n      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        document.getElementById(id)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: \"smooth\" });\n      }, 50);\n      return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);\n    }\n\n    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: \"instant\" });\n  }, [pathname, hash, navigationType]);\n\n  return null;\n}\n",

        "src/components/ui/image.jsx": "import * as React from \"react\"\nimport { useSize } from \"@/hooks/use-size\"\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\"\nimport {\n  buildSrcSet,\n  buildTransformUrl,\n  DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH,\n  getOriginalImageUrl,\n  IMAGE_LOAD_MODE,\n  nextImageLoadMode,\n  parseWixMediaUrl,\n} from \"./image-helpers\"\n\nconst FALLBACK_IMAGE_URL =\n  \"https://static.wixstatic.com/media/12d367_4f26ccd17f8f4e3a8958306ea08c2332~mv2.png\"\n\nconst ImageWrapper = React.forwardRef(({ aspectRatio, className, style, children }, ref) => (\n  <span\n    ref={ref}\n    className={cn(\"inline-block relative\", className)}\n    style={{ aspectRatio, ...style }}\n  >\n    {children}\n  </span>\n))\nImageWrapper.displayName = \"ImageWrapper\"\n\nconst ResponsiveImage = React.forwardRef(\n  ({ parsed, fittingType, focalPoint, quality, className, style, aspectRatio, onLoad, ...props }, parentRef) => {\n    const wrapperRef = React.useRef(null)\n    const imgRef = React.useRef(null)\n    const size = useSize(wrapperRef)\n    const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false)\n\n    React.useImperativeHandle(parentRef, () => imgRef.current)\n\n    // Reset the blur-up when the underlying image changes.\n    React.useEffect(() => {\n      setLoaded(false)\n    }, [parsed.baseUrl])\n\n    const crop = fittingType !== \"fit\"\n    // `size` is null exactly once: the pre-measurement first render, which we\n    // never let reach the network (see below — useSize measures before paint).\n    // A *measured* zero (content-sized wrapper with no CSS dimensions) falls\n    // back to a fixed transform width so the image itself can size the box.\n    const options = size && {\n      width: size.width || DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH,\n      height: size.height ? size.height : undefined,\n      crop,\n      focalPoint: crop ? focalPoint : undefined,\n      quality,\n    }\n\n    // Both layers render only once the container is measured, so the first\n    // URL the browser ever fetches is already the right size — never a\n    // DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH guess that gets replaced a frame later (a\n    // wasted full-size download per image). useSize measures in\n    // useLayoutEffect, so nothing is lost: measurement lands before the\n    // first paint.\n    return (\n      <ImageWrapper ref={wrapperRef} aspectRatio={aspectRatio} className={className} style={style}>\n        {/* Tiny blurred placeholder (a few hundred bytes) covering the main\n            image's load time. Same crop shape and focal anchor as the main\n            image — fp_ is relative to the crop box, so a square or centered\n            placeholder would blur-preview a different region. */}\n        {options && !loaded && (\n          <img\n            src={buildTransformUrl(parsed, {\n              ...options,\n              width: 20,\n              height: options.height\n                ? Math.max(1, Math.round((20 * options.height) / options.width))\n                : undefined,\n              quality: 20,\n            })}\n            alt=\"\"\n            aria-hidden=\"true\"\n            className=\"w-full h-full inset-0 absolute\"\n            style={{\n              objectFit: fittingType === \"fit\" ? \"contain\" : \"cover\",\n              filter: \"blur(10px)\",\n              transform: \"scale(1.1)\",\n            }}\n          />\n        )}\n        {options && (\n          <img\n            ref={imgRef}\n            src={buildTransformUrl(parsed, options)}\n            srcSet={buildSrcSet(parsed, options)}\n            loading=\"lazy\"\n            className={cn(\n              \"w-full h-full inset-0 absolute\",\n              fittingType === \"fit\" ? \"object-contain\" : \"object-cover\"\n            )}\n            onLoad={(e) => {\n              setLoaded(true)\n              onLoad?.(e)\n            }}\n            {...props}\n          />\n        )}\n      </ImageWrapper>\n    )\n  }\n)\nResponsiveImage.displayName = \"ResponsiveImage\"\n\n/**\n * Image with built-in Wix Media Platform support: canonical public images on\n * media.base44.com and static.wixstatic.com/media are resized to the rendered\n * container per device pixel ratio and re-encoded to WebP; `fittingType=\"fill\"`\n * crops server-side, optionally anchored at a focal point. Other URLs render\n * as a plain <img>. Failed transforms retry the original URL; only a broken\n * original swaps to the generic fallback image.\n */\nconst Image = React.forwardRef(\n  (\n    {\n      src,\n      fittingType = \"fill\",\n      originWidth,\n      originHeight,\n      focalPointX,\n      focalPointY,\n      quality = 90,\n      onError,\n      ...props\n    },\n    ref\n  ) => {\n    const parsedSource = src && src !== FALLBACK_IMAGE_URL ? parseWixMediaUrl(src) : null\n    const initialMode = parsedSource ? IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.OPTIMIZED : IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.ORIGINAL\n    const [loadState, setLoadState] = React.useState({ src, mode: initialMode })\n    const mode = loadState.src === src ? loadState.mode : initialMode\n\n    React.useEffect(() => {\n      setLoadState({ src, mode: initialMode })\n    }, [src, initialMode])\n\n    const handleError = (event) => {\n      if (mode === IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.FALLBACK) return\n      const nextMode = nextImageLoadMode(mode)\n      setLoadState({ src, mode: nextMode })\n      if (nextMode === IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.FALLBACK) onError?.(event)\n    }\n\n    const imageProps = {\n      ...props,\n      onError: handleError,\n    }\n\n    if (!src) {\n      // Renders as a real <img> (not a <div>) — the visual editor's\n      // click-to-edit toolbar keys its \"Replace Image\" action off the DOM\n      // tag being `img`, so a placeholder div would be unrecoverable in the\n      // editor. FALLBACK_IMAGE_URL doubles as the \"no image chosen\" graphic.\n      return <img ref={ref} src={FALLBACK_IMAGE_URL} {...imageProps} data-empty-image />\n    }\n\n    // A failed transform retries the underlying original as a plain image.\n    // Only a failure of that original advances to the generic fallback.\n    const parsed = mode === IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.OPTIMIZED ? parsedSource : null\n\n    if (!parsed) {\n      const isErrorMode = mode === IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.FALLBACK\n      const imageSrc = isErrorMode ? FALLBACK_IMAGE_URL : getOriginalImageUrl(src, parsedSource)\n      return (\n        <img ref={ref} src={imageSrc} {...imageProps} data-error-image={isErrorMode || undefined} />\n      )\n    }\n\n    const focalPoint =\n      typeof focalPointX === \"number\" && typeof focalPointY === \"number\"\n        ? { x: focalPointX, y: focalPointY }\n        : undefined\n    // Origin dimensions are optional — when known they stabilize layout via\n    // the wrapper's aspect-ratio before the image loads.\n    const aspectRatio =\n      originWidth && originHeight ? `${originWidth} / ${originHeight}` : undefined\n\n    return (\n      <ResponsiveImage\n        ref={ref}\n        parsed={parsed}\n        fittingType={fittingType}\n        focalPoint={focalPoint}\n        quality={quality}\n        aspectRatio={aspectRatio}\n        {...imageProps}\n      />\n    )\n  }\n)\nImage.displayName = \"Image\"\n\nexport { Image }\n",

        "src/components/ui/use-toast.jsx": "// Inspired by react-hot-toast library\nimport { useState, useEffect } from \"react\";\n\nconst TOAST_LIMIT = 20;\nconst TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;\n\nconst actionTypes = {\n  ADD_TOAST: \"ADD_TOAST\",\n  UPDATE_TOAST: \"UPDATE_TOAST\",\n  DISMISS_TOAST: \"DISMISS_TOAST\",\n  REMOVE_TOAST: \"REMOVE_TOAST\",\n};\n\nlet count = 0;\n\nfunction genId() {\n  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_VALUE;\n  return count.toString();\n}\n\nconst toastTimeouts = new Map();\n\nconst addToRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {\n  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {\n    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);\n    dispatch({\n      type: actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST,\n      toastId,\n    });\n  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);\n\n  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);\n};\n\nconst _clearFromRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {\n  const timeout = toastTimeouts.get(toastId);\n  if (timeout) {\n    clearTimeout(timeout);\n    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);\n  }\n};\n\nexport const reducer = (state, action) => {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case actionTypes.ADD_TOAST:\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),\n      };\n\n    case actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST:\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>\n          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t\n        ),\n      };\n\n    case actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST: {\n      const { toastId } = action;\n\n      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,\n      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity\n      if (toastId) {\n        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);\n      } else {\n        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {\n          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);\n        });\n      }\n\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>\n          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined\n            ? {\n                ...t,\n                open: false,\n              }\n            : t\n        ),\n      };\n    }\n    case actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST:\n      if (action.toastId === undefined) {\n        return {\n          ...state,\n          toasts: [],\n        };\n      }\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),\n      };\n  }\n};\n\nconst listeners = [];\n\nlet memoryState = { toasts: [] };\n\nfunction dispatch(action) {\n  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);\n  listeners.forEach((listener) => {\n    listener(memoryState);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction toast({ ...props }) {\n  const id = genId();\n\n  const update = (props) =>\n    dispatch({\n      type: actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST,\n      toast: { ...props, id },\n    });\n\n  const dismiss = () =>\n    dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId: id });\n\n  dispatch({\n    type: actionTypes.ADD_TOAST,\n    toast: {\n      ...props,\n      id,\n      open: true,\n      onOpenChange: (open) => {\n        if (!open) dismiss();\n      },\n    },\n  });\n\n  return {\n    id,\n    dismiss,\n    update,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction useToast() {\n  const [state, setState] = useState(memoryState);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    listeners.push(setState);\n    return () => {\n      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);\n      if (index > -1) {\n        listeners.splice(index, 1);\n      }\n    };\n  }, [state]);\n\n  return {\n    ...state,\n    toast,\n    dismiss: (toastId) => dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId }),\n  };\n}\n\nexport { useToast, toast }; ",

event.key === SIDEBAR_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT &&\n        (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)\n      ) {\n        event.preventDefault()\n        toggleSidebar()\n      }\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener(\"keydown\", handleKeyDown)\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"keydown\", handleKeyDown);\n  }, [toggleSidebar])\n\n  // We add a state so that we can do data-state=\"expanded\" or \"collapsed\".\n  // This makes it easier to style the sidebar with Tailwind classes.\n  const state = open ? \"expanded\" : \"collapsed\"\n\n  const contextValue = toggleSidebar])\n\n  return (\n    (<SidebarContext.Provider value={contextValue}>\n      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={0}>\n        <div\n          style={\n            {\n              \"--sidebar-width\": SIDEBAR_WIDTH,\n              \"--sidebar-width-icon\": SIDEBAR_WIDTH_ICON,\n              ...style\n            }\n          }\n          className={cn(\n            \"group/sidebar-wrapper flex min-h-svh w-full has-[[data-variant=inset]]:bg-sidebar\",\n            className\n          )}\n          ref={ref}\n          {...props}>\n          {children}\n        </div>\n      </TooltipProvider>\n    </SidebarContext.Provider>)\n  );\n})\nSidebarProvider.displayName = \"SidebarProvider\"\n\nconst Sidebar = React.forwardRef((\n  {\n    side = \"left\",\n         "src/components/ui/carousel.jsx": "import * as React from \"react\"\nimport useEmblaCarousel from \"embla-carousel-react\";\nimport { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight } from \"lucide-react\"\n\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\"\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\"\n\nconst CarouselContext = React.createContext(null)\n\nfunction useCarousel() {\n  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext)\n\n  if (!context) {\n    throw new Error(\"useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />\")\n  }\n\n  return context\n}\n\nconst Carousel = React.forwardRef((\n  {\n    orientation = \"horizontal\",\n    opts,\n    setApi,\n    plugins,\n    className,\n    children,\n    ...props\n  },\n  ref\n) => {\n  const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel({\n    ...opts,\n    axis: orientation === \"horizontal\" ? \"x\" : \"y\",\n  }, plugins)\n  const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false)\n  const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false)\n\n  const onSelect = React.useCallback((api) => {\n    if (!api) {\n      return\n    }\n\n    setCanScrollPrev(api.canScrollPrev())\n    setCanScrollNext(api.canScrollNext())\n  }, [])\n\n  const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => {\n    api?.scrollPrev()\n  }, [api])\n\n  const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => {\n    api?.scrollNext()\n  }, [api])\n\n  const handleKeyDown = React.useCallback((event) => {\n    if (event.key === \"ArrowLeft\") {\n      event.preventDefault()\n      scrollPrev()\n    } else if (event.key === \"ArrowRight\") {\n      event.preventDefault()\n      scrollNext()\n    }\n  }, [scrollPrev, scrollNext])\n\n  React.useEffect(() => {\n    if (!api || !setApi) {\n      return\n    }\n\n    setApi(api)\n  }, [api, setApi])\n\n  React.useEffect(() => {\n    if (!api) {\n      return\n    }\n\n    onSelect(api)\n    api.on(\"reInit\", onSelect)\n    api.on(\"select\", onSelect)\n\n    return () => {\n      api?.off(\"select\", onSelect)\n    };\n  }, [api, onSelect])\n\n  return (\n    (<CarouselContext.Provider\n      value={{\n        carouselRef,\n        api: api,\n        opts,\n        orientation:\n          orientation || (opts?.axis === \"y\" ? \"vertical\" : \"horizontal\"),\n        scrollPrev,\n        scrollNext,\n        canScrollPrev,\n        canScrollNext,\n      }}>\n      <div\n        ref={ref}\n        onKeyDownCapture={handleKeyDown}\n        className={cn(\"relative\", className)}\n        role=\"region\"\n        aria-roledescription=\"carousel\"\n        {...props}>\n        {children}\n      </div>\n    </CarouselContext.Provider>)\n  );\n})\nCarousel.displayName = \"Carousel\"\n\nconst CarouselContent = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {\n  const { carouselRef, orientation } = useCarousel()\n\n  return (\n    (<div ref={carouselRef} className=\"overflow-hidden\">\n      <div\n        ref={ref}\n        className={cn(\n          \"flex\",\n          orientation === \"horizontal\" ? \"-ml-4\" : \"-mt-4 flex-col\",\n          className\n        )}\n        {...props} />\n    </div>)\n  );\n})\nCarouselContent.displayName = \"CarouselContent\"\n\nconst CarouselItem = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {\n  const { orientation } = useCarousel()\n\n  return (\n    (<div\n      ref={ref}\n      role=\"group\"\n      aria-roledescription=\"slide\"\n      className={cn(\n        \"min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full\",\n        orientation === \"horizontal\" ?         "src/components/ui/image-helpers.js": "const WIX_MEDIA_HOSTS = {\n  \"media.base44.com\": \"/images/public/\",\n  \"static.wixstatic.com\": \"/media/\",\n}\n\nexport const DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH = 1024\nexport const IMAGE_LOAD_MODE = {\n  OPTIMIZED: \"optimized\",\n  ORIGINAL: \"original\",\n  FALLBACK: \"fallback\",\n}\n\nconst DEVICE_PIXEL_RATIOS = [1, 2, 3]\nconst MAX_DIMENSION = 6000\n\n/** Returns transform metadata only for canonical public Wix image URLs. */\nexport function parseWixMediaUrl(src) {\n  try {\n    const url = new URL(src)\n    if (\n      url.protocol !== \"https:\" ||\n      url.username ||\n      url.password ||\n      (url.port && url.port !== \"443\")\n    ) {\n      return null\n    }\n\n    const pathPrefix = WIX_MEDIA_HOSTS[url.hostname]\n    if (!pathPrefix) return null\n\n    const transformed = url.pathname.match(/^(.*)\\/v1\\/(?:fill|fit)\\/[^/]+\\/[^/]+$/i)\n    const basePath = transformed ? transformed[1] : url.pathname\n    const filename = basePath.split(\"/\").pop()\n    if (\n      !basePath.startsWith(pathPrefix) ||\n      !filename ||\n      !/\\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(filename) ||\n      /\\.svg$/i.test(filename)\n    ) {\n      return null\n    }\n\n    return { baseUrl: `${url.origin}${basePath}`, filename }\n  } catch {\n    return null\n  }\n}\n\nconst clampDim = (n) => Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(n), 1), MAX_DIMENSION)\nconst clamp01 = (n) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, n))\n\nexport function buildTransformUrl(\n  { baseUrl, filename },\n  { width, height, crop, focalPoint, quality }\n) {\n  const params = [`w_${clampDim(width)}`, `h_${clampDim(height || width)}`]\n  if (crop) {\n    params.push(\n      focalPoint\n        ? `fp_${clamp01(focalPoint.x).toFixed(2)}_${clamp01(focalPoint.y).toFixed(2)}`\n        : \"al_c\"\n    )\n  }\n  params.push(`q_${quality}`, \"usm_0.66_1.00_0.01\", \"enc_webp\", \"quality_auto\")\n  const outputName = /\\.gif$/i.test(filename)\n    ? filename\n    : filename.replace(/\\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, \"\") + \".webp\"\n  return `${baseUrl}/v1/${crop ? \"fill\" : \"fit\"}/${params.join(\",\")}/${outputName}`\n}\n\nexport function buildSrcSet(parsed, options) {\n  return DEVICE_PIXEL_RATIOS.map(\n    (dpr) =>\n      `${buildTransformUrl(parsed, {\n        ...options,\n        width: options.width * dpr,\n        height: options.height ? options.height * dpr : undefined,\n      })} ${dpr}x`\n  ).join(\", \")\n}\n\nexport function getOriginalImageUrl(src, parsed) {\n  return parsed?.baseUrl || src\n}\n\nexport function nextImageLoadMode(mode) {\n  return mode === IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.OPTIMIZED\n    ? IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.ORIGINAL\n    : IMAGE_LOAD_MODE.FALLBACK\n}\n",

        "src/components/contact/ContactDialog.jsx": "import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogDescription, DialogFooter\n} from '@/components/ui/dialog';\nimport { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';\nimport { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';\nimport { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';\nimport { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';\nimport { Send } from 'lucide-react';\n\nexport default function ContactDialog({ open, onOpenChange, company }) {\n  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', email: '', message: '' });\n  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);\n  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);\n\n  const handleChange = (k) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [k]: e.target.value });\n\n  const handleSubmit = (e) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!form.name || !form.email || !form.message) return;\n    setSending(true);\n    const to = company?.email || '';\n    const subject = encodeURIComponent(`Kontaktanfrage von ${form.name}`);\n    const body = encodeURIComponent(\n      `Name: ${form.name}\\nE-Mail: ${form.email}\\n\\nNachricht:\\n${form.message}`\n    );\n    if (to) {\n      window.location.href = `mailto:${to}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;\n    }\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      setSent(true);\n      setForm({ name: '', email: '', message: '' });\n      setSending(false);\n    }, 500);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(o) => { onOpenChange(o); if (!o) setTimeout(() => setSent(false), 200); }}>\n      <DialogContent className=\"sm:max-w-md\">\n        <DialogHeader>\n          <DialogTitle>Kontaktanfrage senden</DialogTitle>\n          <DialogDescription>\n            Schreiben Sie uns – wir melden uns schnellstmöglich zurück.\n          </DialogDescription>\n        </DialogHeader>\n\n        {sent ? (\n          <div className=\"py-8 text-center\">\n            <div className=\"w-14 h-14 mx-auto bg-emerald-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-3\">\n              <Send className=\"w-7 h-7 text-emerald-600\" />\n            </div>\n            <p className=\"font-semibold text-slate-900\">Nachricht gesendet!</p>\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1\">Vielen Dank für Ihre Anfrage.</p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"space-y-4\">\n            <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n              <Label htmlFor=\"c-name\">Name</Label>\n              <Input id=\"c-name\" value={form.name} onChange={handleChange('name')} required />\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n              <Label htmlFor=\"c-email\">E-Mail</Label>\n              <Input id=\"c-email\" type=\"email\" value={form.email} onChange={handleChange('email')} required />\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n              <Label htmlFor=\"c-msg\">Nachricht</Label>\n              <Textarea id=\"c-msg\" value={form.message} onChange={handleChange('message')} rows={4} required />\n            </div>\n            <DialogFooter>\n              <Button type=\"submit\" disabled={sending} className=\"bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700\">\n                <Send className=\"w-4 h-4 mr-2\" />\n                {sending ? 'Wird geöffnet…' : 'Senden'}\n              </Button>\n            </DialogFooter>\n          </form>\n        )}\n      </DialogContent>\n    </Dialog>\n  );\n}",
